# Topic-bank coverage and review queues

[All-niche index](ALL_NICHE_TOPIC_BANKS.md) · [Reconciliation report](ALL_NICHE_RECONCILIATION_REPORT.md)

All banks remain proposal drafts. Explicit starter links are editorial cross-references, not verified facts or a measure of total subject completeness. An unmapped starter concept is a review item; it may already be addressed in a broader active scope. Read the actual question and scope before adding another episode. Do not generate a duplicate just to fill a mapping cell.

## Per-niche remaining coverage

### 001 AI + Technology Explainers

[Main bank](001_ai_technology_explainers/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Language and script cases:** The added normalization, transliteration and morphology questions cover distinct missing mechanisms. Select qualified language reviewers and declared datasets; dialect speech and accessibility remain in 068, 103 and 133 rather than cloned language-name episodes.
- **Versioned cases and regional institutions:** Select systems, dates, facilities, jurisdictions and participant groups within the existing energy, rights and deployment questions. Add a new proposal only when the case changes the mechanism, decision or outcome question.
- **Frontier and longitudinal evidence:** Memory, adaptation, reasoning and physical learning already have active questions. Evaluate newly documented mechanisms and longer-term deployment outcomes as evidence becomes available; a product launch alone does not warrant another episode.
- **Research and production readiness:** All questions remain proposals requiring actual source discovery, case selection, registered-angle review and claim work. Existing registry IDEA readiness remains unchanged.

### 002 History / Mystery Documentary

[Main bank](002_history_mystery_documentary/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Archives and regional cases:** Further compare underrepresented African, Asian and Indigenous archive traditions, unresolved identifications, and negative findings; avoid duplicating an existing mystery dossier.
- **Sources and readiness:** Inspect actual source access, dates, editions, versions, methods and competing evidence. No new source verification, research certification or production approval was performed.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** Before producing an episode, compare related banks and assign one primary home. A lexical duplicate check does not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 003 Ancient India / Civilization

[Main bank](003_ancient_india_civilization/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Regional and unequal visibility:** Deepen site-by-site regional sequences, non-elite households, women's labor, and transmission across Prakrit, Tamil and other language corpora only where new cases differ from existing questions.
- **Sources and readiness:** Inspect actual source access, dates, editions, versions, methods and competing evidence. No new source verification, research certification or production approval was performed.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** Before producing an episode, compare related banks and assign one primary home. A lexical duplicate check does not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 004 Sanatan / Hindu Mythology

[Main bank](004_sanatan_hindu_mythology/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Textual and living variation:** Select exact Sanskrit and regional-language editions, local oral traditions and deity-form interpretations; keep theological disagreements attributed and avoid asserting a single universal canon.
- **Sources and readiness:** Inspect actual source access, dates, editions, versions, methods and competing evidence. No new source verification, research certification or production approval was performed.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** Before producing an episode, compare related banks and assign one primary home. A lexical duplicate check does not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 005 Documentary Storytelling

[Main bank](005_documentary_storytelling/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Contributor-led case studies:** Develop a consented real-world example for BANK-005-122 and further cases involving cross-language editing, community-controlled release and long-term correction; the current question is a proposal, not a documented participant case.
- **Sources and readiness:** Bounded Apollo 11, U.S. fair-use and early NFB notes inspect primary records, but film-shot provenance, an actual excerpt review and film viewing remain open. Inspect source versions, methods and competing evidence for other proposals; no production approval was performed.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** The legacy true-crime and wildlife pitches now identify niches 010 and 067 as primary subject homes. Compare remaining related banks before producing an episode; lexical duplicate checks do not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 006 Science & Big Questions

[Main bank](006_science_big_questions/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Frontier evidence and competing models:** The JADES, delayed-choice quantum-eraser, Wow! and CCC cases identify original records or competing analyses; inspect their methods before scripting. The other frontier proposals still need a named model, mathematical formulation or actual experiment and primary evidence.
- **Sources and readiness:** Bounded JWST, quantum-eraser, Wow! and CCC notes inspect original reports and selected follow-ups; most proposals still need source access, dates, versions, methods and competing evidence. No research certification or production approval was performed.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** The broad simulation pitch now identifies Time & Reality (055) as its primary home. Before production, compare other related banks and assign one primary home; lexical duplicate checks do not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 007 AI Tools / AI Tutorials

[Main bank](007_ai_tools_ai_tutorials/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Versioned workflow evidence:** Choose current named tool versions and run the proposed prompt, document-to-audio, local-server, voice-agent, prompt-injection, screen-reader, multilingual, export and migration tests; retain failure cases and maintenance dates. No live local-runtime test was possible in the current environment.
- **Sources and readiness:** Vendor documentation on Ollama and LM Studio data paths and FTC voice-scam guidance were inspected in bounded notes; most proposals still need actual versioned runs, independent observation and source checks. No research certification or production approval was performed.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** The legacy RAG, prompt-injection and agent-framework pitches now name niche 001 as their conceptual home, while niche 007 retains hands-on workflow tests. Compare further related banks before production; lexical duplicate checks do not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 008 Personal Finance

[Main bank](008_personal_finance/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Jurisdiction and household variation:** Choose dated product documents, tax jurisdictions, irregular-income scenarios, disability and caregiving contexts; all calculations remain educational assumptions until checked.
- **Sources and readiness:** A bounded CFPB source review supports the 50/30/20 heuristic boundary for BANK-008-134. Product, tax, investment, housing and other subject premises remain unverified; inspect current primary documents, methods and competing evidence before scripting or production approval.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** Before producing an episode, compare related banks and assign one primary home. A lexical duplicate check does not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 009 Psychology / Human Behavior

[Main bank](009_psychology_human_behavior/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Populations and construct validity:** Expand specific non-Western and multilingual study comparisons, measurement invariance, replications and conflicting theories without treating psychological constructs as diagnoses.
- **Sources and readiness:** A bounded primary-paper review covers the 1963 Milgram condition for BANK-009-142. Other experiment, intervention and construct premises remain unverified; inspect original methods, sampling, later variants and competing evidence before scripting or production approval.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** Before producing an episode, compare related banks and assign one primary home. A lexical duplicate check does not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 010 True Crime Documentary

[Main bank](010_true_crime_documentary/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Access and affected-community evidence:** Assess public court access, privacy restrictions, unresolved evidence disputes, corrections and victim/community participation for each named jurisdiction and case.
- **Sources and readiness:** A bounded FBI and museum public-source review covers the Gardner theft boundary for BANK-010-126. Other named cases and method claims remain unverified; inspect current official case status, primary records, affected-community evidence and competing interpretations before scripting or production approval.
- **Neighboring-niche boundary review:** Before producing an episode, compare related banks and assign one primary home. A lexical duplicate check does not establish semantic uniqueness across every pair.

### 011 Horror / Mystery Stories

### 281 Ayurveda

[Main bank](281_ayurveda/IN_DEPTH_TOPIC_BANK.md)

- **Legacy cases and formats:** Every preserved playlist, queue and trend reference now has an editorial disposition and scope referral. Named plant/recipe/season variants and field visits still need a distinct case, permission and sources before becoming episodes; short-form packaging alone is not new subject coverage.
- **Regional and textual case selection:** Foundational terms, named-text comparisons, colonial institutions and household transmission now have explicit questions. Choose exact passages, editions, language assistance, regions and practitioner accounts rather than imply a single tradition-wide answer.
- **Evidence and qualified review:** Preparation, population, translated outcomes, reporting and public-claim questions are scoped. Actual trial records, official dated standards, laboratory data and qualified clinical interpretation remain research work; no clinical protocol or product certification is supplied.

## Cross-cutting open work

- Choose exact cases, versions, places, populations, editions and dates where the proposal requires them.
- Continue regional, linguistic, chronological and participant depth only when it creates a distinct question.
- Compare neighboring niches and keep one primary home for the same episode.
- Source discovery, factual verification, source rights, registered-angle scope review, idea readiness, scripts, media and publication remain separate work.
